INSERT INTO ee_control VALUES (false);
DO $check$
DECLARE n integer;
BEGIN
 IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.organizations WHERE id='2c254edf-2119-4079-b09e-dc672e32c1f9' AND name='by Artifact Studio Jewelry') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Organization mismatch'; END IF;
 SELECT jsonb_array_length(doc) INTO n FROM ee_payload;
 IF n<>30 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Expected 30 models'; END IF;
 SELECT count(*) INTO n FROM ee_payload, jsonb_array_elements(doc) x, jsonb_array_elements(x->'variants') v;
 IF n<>2970 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Expected 2970 variants'; END IF;
 SELECT count(distinct v->>0) INTO n FROM ee_payload, jsonb_array_elements(doc) x, jsonb_array_elements(x->'variants') v;
 IF n<>2970 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Duplicate SKU'; END IF;
 IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ee_payload,jsonb_array_elements(doc) x,jsonb_array_elements(x->'variants') v WHERE (v->>3)::integer<=0 OR (v->>2)::numeric<=0 OR length(v->>0)>32) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Invalid variant'; END IF;
 IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ee_payload,jsonb_array_elements(doc) x JOIN public.products p ON p.id=(x->>'id')::uuid OR (p.org_id='2c254edf-2119-4079-b09e-dc672e32c1f9' AND p.sku=x->>'sku') WHERE p.org_id<>'2c254edf-2119-4079-b09e-dc672e32c1f9' OR p.id<>(x->>'id')::uuid OR p.status<>'draft' OR p.etsy_listing_id IS NOT NULL OR p.listing_metadata->>'sourcePackage' IS DISTINCT FROM '2026-10-09-artifact-evil-eye') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Product collision or linked/live product; no overwrite'; END IF;
 IF EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM ee_payload,jsonb_array_elements(doc) x,jsonb_array_elements(x->'variants') v JOIN public.product_variants pv ON pv.org_id='2c254edf-2119-4079-b09e-dc672e32c1f9' AND pv.sku=v->>0 WHERE pv.product_id IS DISTINCT FROM (x->>'id')::uuid OR pv.etsy_listing_id IS NOT NULL OR pv.properties IS DISTINCT FROM v->1 OR pv.price_cents IS DISTINCT FROM (v->>3)::integer OR pv.weight_grams IS DISTINCT FROM (v->>2)::numeric) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'Variant collision; no overwrite'; END IF;
END $check$;
